The lane scene `deploy` of `crates/app/tests/m6c_lane.rs` finds a vehicle type that deploys in place, clicks it, and writes `local-lane/m6c/deploy-before.png`, `deploy-transition.png`, `deploy-deployed.png` and `deploy-packed.png`.

Revision 7 (taken in at step 1c) changes claim 5: a landing-first vehicle that is hovering with a movement commitment is rerouted by the deploy order's destination clear (`orders::apply_other` calls `jump::destination_cleared`, S-0502 claim 24) and keeps the replacement point, or keeps the preceding point and commitment when the clear is a suppressed unchanged assignment; an equal horizontal point lets the landing request produce descent, a different one makes it cruise first; with no commitment the request does not wake an idle hover (`jump::request_landing` sets no commitment; tests `claims_s0517_r7.rs`). There are no provisional rules of the deployable units left: Q-0982 is answered by S-0517 revision 6 claim 7 (a transition completes at t₀ + (E − 1) d + 1, an undeploy at t₀ + (E − 2) d + 1, counted with the base stage delay: `inplace::stage_delay` has no normalisation) and Q-0975's last use by S-0502 claim 23's last row (`inplace::landing_first_take_off`: the type's own class, winged counted as track, its own zone, the centre's reach region read for its deck, deck mode when on a deck, no height condition, bridges allowed).

## Rendering of aircraft attitude (M6c)

S-0105 revision 20 (answers Q-0950), claims 41 and 42, 3, 26 and 30, the importer rows. Presentation only; the simulation is read, never changed.

- **Rocket body (claim 41).** `render::voxel::rocket_body_pose` poses the body as *V · Rz(θ_body) · Ry(−p)* (`rocket_body_local`), *p* the missile's current single-precision pitch, used directly with F1's table trigonometry (no pitch facings), and the shadow as *V · Rz(θ_body)* whatever the pitch. The pitch reaches the viewer through `sim::rocket::drawing` (new, read-only: the pitch and the selected set's initial and final fractions as single bit patterns) and `Attitude::Rocket` on `Mobile` (`live::attitude`). The reuse classes are `RocketPitch` (`rocket_pitch_class`): zero is level; a pitch equal to RZ₅₃(Pᵢ × c) widened (c = 1.5707963267948966), compared without narrowing the product, is initial; else equal to RZ₂₄(RZ₅₃(P𝒇 × c)) is final; every other pitch is fresh. The image key (`ImageKey::RocketBody`) is the 32-step heading, the frame and the class. A fresh pitch is cached under the table step of its rotation: the pixels are those of a fresh draw and only the memory of the scene's images is bounded (the scene keeps every image added, `cached_part`).
- **Flying aircraft (claim 42).** `Attitude::Flying` carries the signed height (claim 40's a), the current speed fraction (`AirState::cur`), the sign of an unfinished aiming-facing turn (`Object::turret`, positive clockwise) and whether the body is crashing. `render::voxel::flight_attitude` gives the pitch α and bank β: both zero unless the height is strictly positive and the speed fraction strictly above `PitchSpeed` (double precision); α is `PitchAngle` then; β is `RollAngle` (negated for a counterclockwise turn) during an unfinished turn, a discrete state. Defaults 20 degrees, 30 degrees, 0.25 (S-0105 Import mapping; −1 keeps the default; degrees × binary64 π/180 toward zero, `build::rule_angle`); the three keys are `render` rows in the coverage table. The pose is *V · Rz(θ_flight) · Ry(α) · Rx(β)* (`flying_body_pose`) with α and β narrowed to single toward zero, the shadow the heading alone. Key: heading bucket, frame, pitch presence (only with a nonzero configured angle) and bank state (none for a zero configured angle), `ImageKey::FlyingBody`.
- **Heading sources (Terms, claims 3, 30).** A flying-locomotor aircraft's body and shadow take the **aiming facing** (`Mobile::turret`) rounded to 32 steps, a rocket's body and shadow the body facing, every other body its body facing. The shadow key of a flying body is its heading bucket with no pitch or bank class.
- **Not built.** Crashing flying bodies keep S-0312's attitude rules, which are not drawn; they are drawn level (heading only). A map-only scene (no game) draws aircraft level with the body facing. Slope tilt of aircraft is S-0106's. The reuse observations of MS-31 and MS-32 (a recorder of reuse against fresh draws) have no counterpart here: our cache cannot tell a reused image from a fresh one with the same pixels.

## Simulation (M6c: radiation fields)

S-0525 is built in `glasnost_sim::radiation`; the ledger is [docs/m6-claims/S-0525.md](m6-claims/S-0525.md), the tests are `claims_s0525*.rs` (fixture `rad_common`; the numbers are the spec's own scenarios RF1 to RF12) and, for the importer, `crates/convert/src/sim_radiation_tests.rs`. New state is `World::radiation` (`RadWorld`: the fields in creation order and the cell radiation as a sparse map of soft doubles); save format version 38.

- **Making and adding (claims 1 to 4).** `radiation::detonation(w, weapon, cell)` is called by every projectile detonation (`combat::projectile::explode`: once for a plain shot, once for an airburst's own detonation, once per cluster point; the nine airburst children are marked `Projectile::airburst_child` and leave none), by a death weapon's detonation (`projectile::detonate_weapon`) and so by the nuclear payload and its upward missile. A detonation on the centre cell of a live field tops it up (`top_up`: the new level from the floating-point field level, the removal of F5 from every cell of the square with `quirks.radiation_add_overdraw`, new duration, both timers restarted, the contribution of the new level over the old reach, the light set afresh with the old radius); any other cell makes a new field.
- **Floating point.** Cell radiation, contributions (F1), decay (F3), removal (F5), the level factor (F6) and the light products (F7) use the soft double in the order the formulas name; integers wrap; the distance is the table root of S-0204 with the height of each cell's centre point (`World::surface_at`).
- **Row 13 (claims 5, 6, 11, 14).** `radiation::maintenance` runs in `tick::step` after the storm and before the turn pass, newest field first: time left, a decay step when `t - start >= D_L`, a light step when `t - start >= D_I`, and the end at time left 0 (`quirks.radiation_residue` off takes the rest of the field's contribution). It takes no draws.
- **The hit (claims 8 to 10).** `radiation::hit_turn` runs in `tick::object_turn` after `heal::turn` (the order of claim 11), and since M7b step 1b after the behaviour processing and the damage sparks (S-0616 claim 1); an erasing turn takes it before it returns, for infantry, vehicles and aircraft on the map that are not airborne (`weapon::in_air` for aircraft, the 208-lepton height test for the rest) and not immune, on ticks that are multiples of the application delay: one `damage::apply` hit of trunc(ℓ x k_L) with the radiation-site warhead, no attacker, no house, the no-escape marker.
- **S-0215 claim 31 (immune deployers).** `process::immune_deployed` reads the field level of a field centred on the unit's cell (`radiation::centre_field_level`, claim 7) against R = trunc(W / 3) and fires the secondary weapon at the unit's own cell through the firing check, or drops the target; delays are the deployed-fire frame count or D_r + U(10, 20).
- **Light (claims 13 to 15).** `radiation::lights` lists the live fields' lights (centre cell, radius, intensity, tint after F7, with the snow-map red and blue of -128). The map view is built once with static cell lights and has no dynamic relight (no light change of any kind is drawn: storms and the dominator are the same), so the view draws a green ring of the field's reach instead (`Marker::Field`, our placeholder, `provisional`). The relight itself waits for a dynamic relight in `mapview` (S-0104 claim 12 gives its rules).
- **Importer.** The ten `[Radiation]` keys are `General` fields (`rad_*`, `rules::sim::General`) read by `convert::sim_combat` (`RadColor` as a byte triple with the value in force for unread components); warnings for a delay of 0 or less, a duration multiple of 0 or less, a missing radiation-site warhead, and weapons whose field has no light or level step.
- **Own AI.** See *Own AI (M6c: special abilities)*.
- **Client.** The lane scene `radiation` of `crates/app/tests/m6c_lane.rs` makes a field with an installed radiation weapon, stands three infantry units at the centre, beside it and outside the reach, and writes `local-lane/m6c/radiation-before.png`, `radiation-field.png` and `radiation-damage.png`.

There are no provisional rules of the radiation fields left: Q-0983 is answered by S-0215 revision 51 claim 31 (taken in at step 1c): the own-cell shot of a radiation-immune deployer is made in the processing that decides to fire, with no wait for a fire frame; the own-cell target is dropped in that processing after the shot, and the field exists in the same tick (test `the_own_cell_shot_is_made_in_the_deciding_processing`).

## Simulation (M6c: chrono weapons)

S-0514 is built in `glasnost_sim::temporal`; the ledger is [docs/m6-claims/S-0514.md](m6-claims/S-0514.md), the tests are `claims_s0514.rs` and `claims_s0514_more.rs` (fixture `temporal_common`) and, for the importer, `crates/convert/src/sim_temporal_tests.rs`. New state is `World::temporal` (`TemporalWorld`: the locks by target, the beams by holder, the beams a gunner vehicle carries); save format version 39. Revision 5 (taken in at step 1c) answers Q-0984.

- **Beams and locks (claims 1 to 4, 9, 10, 20 to 22).** A beam is a type's (primary weapon with a temporal warhead, `temporal::type_beam`) unless a gunner vehicle carries it (`hand_over` on the first passenger's entry, `hand_back` on the last one's exit, S-0308 claim 6); a holder has at most one attached (`beams`). `combat::projectile::special_of` selects the one special effect of a detonation by claim 20's table (takeover `MindControl`, bomb planting, electric charging, parasite attachment, temporal erasure, `IsLocomotor`, air-strike designation, `directrocker` against a vehicle target, bomb disarming, disguise-making, nuke-making: the first enabled row wins; takeover, locomotor replacement and direct rocking are selected only, their effects are seams; a selected row replaces ordinary damage and every lower row, built or not; radiation and the shared presentation stay), and for the erasing row `detonation_inner` skips the ordinary area damage and calls `temporal::deliver` with the firer and the shot's target: a cell or a non-combat object only lets the firer's old beam go, an object outside the ground render layer (`ground_layer`: a flying or jumpjet unit above the ground) does nothing, and for an infantry unit, vehicle, aircraft or building the old beam lets go, the side effects of claim 2 happen (spawner slots cleared; `quirks.erasure_refused_hit_side_effects`), the refusals of claim 3 apply (not warpable, protected, a vehicle in its factory's cell, a locked firer) and a lock is made: the first lock sets *R* = 10 x strength and switches a building off (`quirks.erasure_release_switches_on` at the last release), later locks join.
- **The erasure update (claims 7, 8, 11, 17, 18).** `temporal::turn`, called in `tick::object_turn` before behaviour processing: the sparkle on global ticks divisible by 24, a mobile target drops its target and destination, an open-topped leader beyond *W* cells lets go with no reduction (F4 with the table root; `quirks.erasure_distance_leader_only`), then *R* falls by the sum of the raw damages of the leader and the 51 newest joiners (`weapon::would_use` with no target); a target whose *R* is at most 0 completes. A mobile target skips the rest of its turn; a building skips `process::turn` and the delayed shot (`building_erased`).
- **Completion (claims 12, 13).** The warp-away animation, `garrison::erased` for a building, the direct experience gain of F5 for a trainable leader (`veterancy::gain` with the target's and the leader's own cost, `quirks.erasure_double_experience`), the ordinary credit and loss (`damage::credit`, hit with the leader as attacker), the destruction raises, then `objects::remove` (passengers die with no killer, slaves are freed) and the attackers fall back to rest.
- **Releases (claims 14 to 16).** `temporal::holder_turn` (a new cell), `process::fall_back` (revision 5: the release belongs to the common effects of S-0215 claim 13, at most once per actor per tick, and an aircraft's early exits skip it; the stop order, clearing a target and processing stop or sleep release nothing), `objects::remove` (`temporal::leaving`: a removed target idles its attackers, a removed attacker lets go), `victory::destroy_all` (`detach_for_destruction`, claim 15); clearing an attacker's target alone releases nothing (claim 16). The firing check answers *rearming* for a temporal weapon whose beam holds the target (claim 19).
- **Being erased.** `superweapons::being_erased` is now the union of a lock and the warp's leaving stage, so every reader (damage immunity of S-0302 claim 8, selection, power, radar, garrison entry, engineer entry, cloak) sees it.
- **Importer.** `Warpable` (unit and building types, absent: yes), `[General] WarpAway` and `ChronoSparkle1`, `[CombatDamage] OpenToppedWarpDistance` (absent: 5 cells); the four `erasure_*` quirks are consulted and tested in both settings.
- **Seams.** First-lock notices (claim 5), freeing controlled units and the beam release on mind control (S-0601, `temporal::control_changed`), the bunker redirection (S-0606), the building damage-fire sparkle positions (not imported).
- **Own AI.** See *Own AI (M6c: special abilities)*.
- **Client.** The lane scene `chrono` of `crates/app/tests/m6c_lane.rs` finds an erasing type and a plain vehicle type by their rules flags, clicks the target as a person would, and writes `local-lane/m6c/chrono-before.png`, `chrono-erasing.png` (the lock with the sparkle animation) and `chrono-erased.png`.

Provisional rules of the chrono weapons, each tied to a request (Q-0984 is answered by revision 5 and its three interim rows are retired: claim 20's table, claim 14's fall-back release, claims 21 and 22 for a firer with no beam and a boarding holder; the importer reads `MindControl` and `directrocker` as warhead flags for the selection). Q-1032 is answered by S-0614 revision 9 (step 1e): its interim row is retired, claim 24 is built with `quirks.parasite_suppressed_latch_strands_unit`. No interim rule is left.

## Simulation (M6c: linked weapons)

S-0518 is built in `glasnost_sim::linked`; the ledger is [docs/m6-claims/S-0518.md](m6-claims/S-0518.md), the tests are `claims_s0518.rs` and `claims_s0518_charge.rs` (fixture `linked_common`) and, for the importer (F4 included), `crates/convert/src/sim_linked_tests.rs`. New state is in `CombatState` (a master's support count, a supporter's pending beam, a building's charger list and overpowered flag, a unit's charging mark) and `Projectile::multiplier`; save format version 40.

- **Prism support (claims 1 to 10).** A building of the prism type (`[General] PrismType`) without the SAM flag and without installed upgrades takes `linked::master_path` in `behave::building_process` when its attack gets *can fire*: `linked::recruit` picks the nearest eligible supporter (claim 2's tests; F2 distance with the integer root; the reach is the slot-1 range) and returns, or the master's own delayed shot starts (`start_shot`, always, whatever the animation flag). The supporter's pending beam (`support_beam`, due max(d - 1, 0) ticks later) is drawn in `linked::supporter_turn` (an `Effect::SupportBeam`, drawing only) with its rearm countdown set to exactly the support rearm, and the firing check answers *rearming* meanwhile. `linked::master_shot` fires slot 0 through the check; a projectile launched with a positive count carries *M* (F1, `Projectile::multiplier`) and the count resets; the supplied area damage is ⌊*D*ₜ *M* / 256⌋ (`scaled_damage`). The resets of claim 10 are in `building_process`; the dropped-shot case is `quirks.prism_count_survives_dropped_shot`.
- **Charging (claims 11 to 15).** `linked::charge_hit` registers an infantry firer of an electric-charge detonation on the building it hit; `building_turn` (in the building's own turn) prunes chargers that no longer aim at an overpowerable building (any building with `quirks.stale_chargers_kept` off) and sets the overpowered state (F3); `econ::power::chargers` reads the list (S-0405 claim 8) and `weapon::choose_slot` rows 10 and 11 read the state and the flags. `linked::start_charging` runs in the *guard* and *area guard* processing of a charger with no target (revision 3, Q-0985: it queues *attack* without starting it, the idle action does not run, and the processing keeps its own delay: guard's rearm remainder, 0 for distributed fire with candidates, or the standard delay with its draw 0 to 2; area guard's rate delay plus a draw 1 to 5, divided by 6 under the close-range condition), and `linked::break_off` at the start of an infantry unit's *attack* processing (`quirks.vehicle_charge_no_break_off`): the range search of `SearchContext::BreakOff` takes no targeting-timer reset draw, the held building is neither tested nor cleared, a result becomes the target and clears the mark, nothing found leaves both. The tie between equally distant supporters goes by acquisition rank (`CombatState::acquired`, `World::acquisitions`: bumped at a building's placement into play and at an ownership change; save format 44). A charger that leaves play leaves every list at once (`objects::remove`).
- **Importer.** `[General]` `PrismType`, `PrismSupportMax`, `PrismSupportDelay`, `PrismSupportDuration`, the modifier through F4 (`convert::layers`: a layer with a general section rescales the held value by 100, `quirks.prism_modifier_reread_scale`, the start value 100) and the building type's `Overpowerable`; the art `DelayedFireDelay` existed.
- **Client.** The view draws support beams and laser weapons' beams by claims 17 to 23 (see *Beams, beam flashes and the magnetic wave* under *Visual review 2026-10-19*); the lane scene `prism` of `crates/app/tests/m6c_lane.rs` builds three prism buildings of the install's prism type, orders the first to attack an enemy vehicle and writes `local-lane/m6c/prism-before.png`, `prism-beams.png` and `prism-after.png`.
- **Own AI.** See *Own AI (M6c: special abilities)*.
- **Seams.** Mind control and charge planting ranking above the charge effect (S-0601, S-0519), prism combinations with the SAM flag or upgrades (the spec's open question). Being drained (S-0608) is built at M7b step 3.3: a drained building does not support (`a_drained_building_does_not_support`).

There are no provisional rules of the linked weapons left: Q-0985 is answered by S-0518 revision 3 (claims 3, 14 and 15, taken in at step 1c).


## Simulation (M6c: terror and strikes)

S-0519 is built in `glasnost_sim::bomb` (planted bombs, charges, suicide shots) and `glasnost_sim::strike` (air-strike designation); the ledger is [docs/m6-claims/S-0519.md](m6-claims/S-0519.md), the tests are `claims_s0519.rs`, `claims_s0519_more.rs` and `claims_s0519_strike.rs` (fixture `terror_common`) and, for the importer, `crates/convert/src/sim_terror_tests.rs`. New state: `CombatState::bomb` (planter, planter's house, plant tick), `CombatState::charge_planter` and `World::strike` (an ordered map of strikes by designator); save format version 41.

- **Planted bombs (claims 2 to 10).** A bomb-planting warhead's projectile calls `bomb::plant` instead of making area hits (`combat::projectile`); `combat::check` rank 9 answers illegal for a carrier or a clean disarm target; the bomb goes off in `bomb::turn`, called from the carrier's own turn after its processing in `tick::object_turn` (F1: tick above *t* + *D*), one detonation at the carrier with the bomb damage and warhead and the planter as attacker while it exists. A killed carrier (`combat::death`) sets it off once, an off-map carrier removes it silently, `objects::remove` drops it, a non-garrisonable building changing owner disarms it (`structures::ownership`), the planter leaving only blanks the planter. Visibility (F2) is `bomb::visible`: used by the engineer cursor row (S-0214 I2) and the client. The detonate order is `Order::Detonate` (S-0213 claim 11), issued in the planter's house's name unless `quirks.detonate_in_planter_name` is off.
- **Suicide (claim 11).** `combat::fire::shot` of a suicide weapon makes no projectile and calls `bomb::suicide`: one hit of the type's strength with the demolition warhead, ignoring defences; the death weapon is S-0312's.
- **Charges (claims 13 and 14).** `structures::entry::arrival` first calls `bomb::charge_arrival` for an infantry unit in *sabotage* whose type has the charges flag (or, with `quirks.charge_needs_type_flag` off, the veterancy ability): the entered-by raise, then the three-row table, the countdown *K* (F4) as the delayed-kill countdown with the planter recorded (its expiry hit's attacker, `combat::damage`), the rearm and the scatter. Revision 3 (taken in at step 1c, answers Q-0986): the rearm is the arrival delay of the second weapon (`combat::fire::arrival_delay`): no second weapon 1 tick; a sonic weapon its rate with no draw; the counter *k* (not *k* + 1, never advanced by arrival) below the burst count one draw over 3 to 5; otherwise the full delay of S-0301 claim 2 with its draw over 0 to 2 and no berserk halving. A spark-particle, fire-particle or railgun second weapon takes the bare-rate row only with a live effect of its kind on the walker; live effects are not built (S-0301 claim 5's gate is a seam), so such a weapon follows the later rows.
- **Strikes (claims 16 to 21).** `strike::designation` is called by the designation warhead's detonation instead of area hits; the readiness test (F5, with `quirks.airstrike_recharge_same_target_only`), `start` (one arrival cell, `n` visiting aircraft placed by the winged nearby search, each in *attack* at the cell of the object's reference point), `cancel` (ammunition, the newest 101 aircraft forget and queue *retreat*), `redirect` (equal ammunition only) and the end of the strike when the last aircraft leaves (`strike::leaving` from `objects::remove`). A change of the designator's target cancels in the tick it applies (revision 3, claim 19, Q-0986 answered): `strike::sweep` looks at every designator after each applied order (`orders::apply_pending`) and after each object's turn (`tick::turn_pass`), and every target change is counted (`CombatState::target_epoch`, bumped by `spawn::set_own_target` and its siblings), so a change to B and back to A still cancels; the designator's own turn and each designation shot look as before. A bomb with no bomb warhead ends silently, detaches and makes no blast (hut demolition stays, claim 22); a suicide with no demolition warhead is still delivered and the importer warns (claim 23). Marks are derived from the strike targets (`strike::marked`). `air::fall_back` no longer exempts a strike aircraft in *retreat*.
- **Importer.** The warhead flags `IvanBomb`, `BombDisarm`, the infantry flags `Ivan`, `Doggie`, the weapon flag `MigAttackCursor`, `BombSight`, the strike keys, the `[CombatDamage]` bomb and charge keys (`C4Delay` held as a decimal, F4 in `bomb::charge_countdown`); warnings for a timed delay below 6, a flicker rate of 0 and a designator with no strike aircraft type. The sound names and `LaserTargetColor` are presentation and not read.
- **Client.** Placeholder markers (`Marker::Bomb`, `Charge`, `Marked`) over carriers the viewer sees, charges counting down and marked buildings; the local lane scene `terror` (screenshots `terror-*.png` in `local-lane/m6c/`).

There are no interim rules of the terror and strike code left: Q-0986 is answered by S-0519 revision 3 (claims 13, 19, 22 and 23).


## Simulation (M6c: crates)

S-0520 is built in `glasnost_sim::crates`; the ledger is [docs/m6-claims/S-0520.md](m6-claims/S-0520.md), the tests are `claims_s0520.rs` and `claims_s0520_effects.rs` (fixture `crate_common`) and, for the importer, `crates/convert/src/sim_crate_tests.rs`. New state: `World::crates` (the crate places by priority 0 … 255 with cell, start tick, lifetime and the trigger mark, and the any-crate mark), the crate rules in `SimRules::crates`, `StartState::bases` (the bases setting kept after the game starts); save format version 42.

- **Placement and expiry (claims 1 to 5).** `crates::place_random` makes at most 1000 attempts, each with the two ranged requests of F5 and the single-cell float or track search (no draw); the first accepted cell (usable area, no overlay) takes the wooden or water overlay (`crates::finish`: `overlays::place_overlay`, the contents in the cell's overlay data, then the lifetime draw of S-0524's formula in the first free place). `place_trigger` (code 108, S-0524 claim 4: one search, no retry, `quirks.trigger_crate_respects_setting`), `place_death` (`combat::death::leave_play` for a destroyed vehicle with `CarriesCrate` or a building with `CrateBeneath`, crates on) and `place_at_start` (S-0412 claim 10's count, in `econ::setup::place_crates`) share it. `crates::update` (tick row 16, after the turn pass) expires the crates whose lifetime ran out, in place order, each replaced by one random placement; with crates off nothing expires unless `quirks.trigger_crate_expiry_while_disabled` lets trigger crates go.
- **Pickup (claims 6 to 11).** `crates::pickup(w, collector, cell)` is called when a walker (`walk::ask`), a driver or ship (`drive::step_check`) or a hover (`hover::step_check`) commits to a step into the cell, at a jumpjet's touchdown (`jump::crate_pickup`) and at a teleporter's landing cell (`teleport`); it returns whether a ground step is blocked (a unit power-up or a collector killed by the tag raise). Gates: the cell's overlay, the passive country, the crate-trigger overlay (event 49 by `triggers::crate_picked_up`, then the any-crate mark). Then the choice (`choose`: fixed contents, or R(1, total) and F1; a forced base unit), the conversions to money (`convert`), the crate's removal and replacement, the effect and the animation 200 leptons above the floor.
- **Effects (claims 12 to 25).** Money, unit (the unit-crate type, the forced unit, the harvester rule with `quirks.crate_harvester_list_prefix`, the random goodie vehicle, the nearby search, the money fallback with `quirks.crate_unit_fallback_unit_argument`), heal all (a healing hit of health - strength to every object of the house in the update order), cloak, explosion (`combat::projectile::explosion_anim_dry` picks the dry animation), napalm, darkness and reveal (`econ::shroud::crate_darkness`, `crate_reveal`), armour, firepower and speed (`quirks.crate_bonus_reaches_limbo`), the nuclear grant (`superweapons::charge::one_time_grant`), veteran (F7), gas (nine area damages, no resource reduction; a default GAS warhead is added to the table when the rules name none), resource (`econ::resource::crate_stage`).
- **Importer.** `[CrateRules]` `CrateRadius` (a value of exactly -1 is dropped), `CrateRegen`, `UnitCrateType`, `WoodCrateImg`, `WaterCrateImg`; `[CombatDamage]` `FlameDamage`; the nineteen `[Powerups]` keys into one table (`convert::sim_econ::crate_import`); `CrateGoodie`, `CrateTrigger`, `CrateBeneath`, `CrateBeneathIsMoney`; the sounds and the campaign-only keys are coverage rows (ignored).
- **Client.** A placeholder box (`Marker::Crate`) on every crate's cell (the view draws overlays from the map only); the power-up animations come through `Effect::Anim`; the local lane scene `crates` (screenshots `crate-*.png` in `local-lane/m6c/`).

Revision 3 (taken in at step 1c) answers Q-0987: the contents of every new random crate are 255 and a trigger's number slot is stored as S-0524 claim 4 says (tests `claims_s0520_r3.rs`); the awarded unit is first offered a direct placement at the crate cell centre that accepts only a clear entry outcome (`crates::clear_for`: `occupancy::vehicle_outcome` with the cell's own level), then the nearby search (the type's class, the *normal* zone, the empty record) whose result gets the same test once, then the money fallback; the stage-picture count of claim 24 is the flat run's *F* (12 for every supported image number), the highest stage 11 and an existing higher stage unchanged; the power-up table is layered (`convert::layers` records a reset event for every omitted key of a layer that holds a surviving `[Powerups]` section, `quirks.override_resets_keys`, and `convert::sim::econ::crate_import` applies it: weight 0, animation the lookup of NONE, water flag and argument kept).

Interim rules waiting for answers: none. Q-1033 and Q-1034 are answered by S-0520 revision 5 and S-0006 revision 20 (step 1e): the reset gives no animation without any lookup, and with `quirks.override_resets_keys` off an unmentioned entry keeps its built-in startup weight (`convert::sim_econ::crate_import`).

## Simulation (M7a: Psychic weapons)

S-0610 (revision 9; built at revision 4, revisions 5 to 8 taken in at M7a step 1b, [docs/m7-claims/step1b-m7a.md](m7-claims/step1b-m7a.md), revision 9 at step 1c, [docs/m7-claims/step1c-m7a.md](m7-claims/step1c-m7a.md)) is built in `glasnost_sim::psychic`; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0610.md](m7-claims/S-0610.md), the tests are `claims_s0610.rs`, `claims_s0610_r8.rs` and, for the importer, `psychic_type_flags` and `psychic_keys`. The berserk state is now a remaining count, `CombatState::berserk` (`Some(t)` while berserk, even at `t` ≤ 0), in place of the earlier end tick; save format version 46.

- **Berserk (claims 3 to 6, 8).** Damage stage 3 (S-0302 claim 10) calls `psychic::enter`: a unit that is not berserk takes T, drops its target and queues hunt; a berserk one only takes the new T; with `quirks.berserk_zero_duration` off a T ≤ 0 hit does nothing. `psychic::countdown_turn` runs in each countdown update (claim 5, revision 8, which answers Q-1180): in the behaviour part of the unit's update (`process::turn`), after the first per-tick start and before the due test, the processing, the per-tick search and every shot, so the expiry update fires no berserk shot and the behaviour it queues is first processed in the next update (S-0215 claim 11's late start may make it current at the end of the expiry update). Erasing updates (`temporal::turn`), warp-only updates (`warp::turn`, from the first pending update through arrival effects) and out-of-play units never reach it, so they keep `t`; in arrival hold it runs after the hold-end test. For a unit that is not an unlimited controller it comes after self-healing; S-0610 revision 9 claim 6 (answering Q-1166 question 2) says that order has no observable effect, so an engine may choose it. An unlimited controller's first per-tick start and countdown come earlier, before its overload update (S-0601 claim 16, below). At `t` ≤ 0 it ends the state, drops the target and queues guard for a human-controlled owner (every player house, AI houses included) or hunt (the neutral and special houses). The firing check answers illegal at rank 4 for a berserk-causing weapon against a psionics-immune object, and for a berserk firer against a berserk-friendly type (`psychic::firing_refused`); the bunker half is the S-0606 seam `bunker_refusal`. `targeting::berserk` now reads the state, so S-0214 claim 15 and rows G1, GC1, R and M apply.
- **Psychic sensor (claims 9 to 12).** Sensors are derived: the on-map buildings a house owns whose type has a psychic-detection radius and that are in operation (`psychic::sensors`). `psychic::intent_lines(w, viewer)` gives one line per enemy unit (the viewer's side of the alliance) whose target (first) or destination is within reach of a sensor (F1: the table root of Δx² + Δy² + Δz² from the sensor's reference point, ≤ 256 × R, no building size), visible or not unless `quirks.psychic_intent_ignores_visibility` is off. `psychic::range_ring` and `psychic::tint` are queries for presentation the view does not draw yet.
- **Psychic reveal (claims 13 to 15).** `superweapons::launch` calls `psychic::reveal`: `econ::shroud::psychic_reveal` (S-0409 claim 17's area reveal without claim 7's height test, radius from `[CombatDamage]` `PsychicRevealRadius`, 3 when absent, for the launching house and those S-0409 claim 2 lets share it) and `SwEvent::RevealSound` at the target cell's centre, which the audio crate plays (`PsychicRevealActivateSound`). No launch announcement, no radar event, no lasting sight.
- **Importer.** `BerserkFriendly` (any type), `HasRadialIndicator` (building), `[CombatDamage]` `PsychicRevealRadius` and `BerzerkAllowed` (read, no effect, claim 16), `[AudioVisual]` `PsychicRevealActivateSound` and `BerserkColor`; the ruleset schema was regenerated.
- **Client.** `Session::draw` draws the local player's intent lines: dashes moving with the real-time clock, a 3 × 3 square in the unit owner's radar colour at both ends (`house_colours`). The local lane scenes `berserk`, `sensor` and `reveal` (`crates/app/tests/m7a_lane.rs`, screenshots in `local-lane/m7a/`).

The late start (S-0215 claim 11, revision 57, taken in at M7a step 1b). An infantry unit or vehicle has one more ready-and-start chance in its update, `process::late_start`. It is called in `tick::object_turn` right after the locomotor step and the passenger sync. That is after the behaviour part (the drop rule and the per-tick search never see what it starts), before the depot retry, the parasite step, the vehicle firing step and turret, and the infantry sequence rules and firing. Its readiness test (`process::ready`) therefore reads the movement state that this update has left (since revision 67 explicitly the current predicate, not a remembered displacement) and the infantry sequence that earlier updates left. It makes a queued behaviour current; an infantry unit that is ready with neither a current nor a queued behaviour first falls back to rest, and the behaviour that queues becomes current there (revision 59, answering Q-1166 question 1; tests `claims_s0215_r59.rs`). A vehicle with both slots empty is left as it is. The behaviour it starts is processed in the unit's next update (claim 3b). Erasing, tunnel and warp-only updates return before it. Buildings keep their second start; aircraft keep their end-of-update start. A behaviour queued during an update (an arrival report, a refusal, a processing that queues without starting) is therefore current at the end of the update if the unit is then ready. The game hashes changed with it.

Seams: S-0606 (bunkered vehicles, claim 3), S-0613 (gas clouds), S-0611 and S-0612 (M7b; `superweapons::launch::yuri_seam` keeps the dominator and the genetic mutator), the berserk tint and range ring drawing, the ready-announcement cancellation (no kind-specific ready announcements yet). Interim rules waiting for answers: none (Q-1166 is answered by S-0215 revision 59 and S-0610 revision 9, step 1c). Tunnels are not built, so their exclusion of the countdown is a seam.

## Simulation (M7a: Mind control)

S-0601 (revision 16; built at revision 5, revisions 6 to 10 taken in at M7a step 1c, [docs/m7-claims/step1c-m7a.md](m7-claims/step1c-m7a.md), revisions 11 and 12 at step 1d, [docs/m7-claims/step1d-m7a.md](m7-claims/step1d-m7a.md), revision 13 at step 1g, revisions 14 to 16 at step 1h, [docs/m7-claims/step1h-m7a.md](m7-claims/step1h-m7a.md): the overload's five spark systems are made, and with no game-wide spark type neither they nor their placement draws happen) is built in `glasnost_sim::mind`; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0601.md](m7-claims/S-0601.md), the tests are `claims_s0601.rs`, `claims_s0601_deploy.rs`, `claims_s0601_r9.rs` and, for the importer, `mind_control_type_and_weapon_fields`, `mind_control_game_wide_values` and `mind_control_warnings`. The state is `World::mind` (`MindWorld`: the controller records by object, the victim records with their controller and original owner, the permanently controlled set); it is saved and hashed. Save format version 47.

- **Controllers and the takeover (claims 1 to 7).** `mind::created` gives a new object a controller record when its type's rookie primary weapon has a takeover warhead (capacity: that weapon's damage; unlimited: its `InfiniteMindControl`). `mind::can_take` is the takeover test, `mind::firing_illegal` the firing check's refusal at rank 9 (`combat::check`), and the detonation path calls `mind::detonation` in place of damage: attack events 6 and then 44 for a tagged target, both caused by the firer (claim 5, revision 9, answering Q-1203; `triggers::judge` judges event 6 true in any raise of its code and event 44 when its value is the house number of the causing object's owner, S-0011 claim 8 revision 23; the event-44 raise looks the tag up again after the event-6 raise and is made on the tag the target then carries, or skipped when it has none: revision 12, answering Q-1150, makes this a departure with no switch, since the original faults there, and `quirks.removed_tag_attack_event` is gone; test `tags::claim_5_removed_tag_skips_event_44`), the test again at landing, then `mind::take` (capacity-1 replacement, original owner, owner change through `set_owner` with `Cause::MindControl`, which now requests the capture or loss announcement for a building on takeover and release, S-0509 claim 12 and S-0701 claim 39, guard reset, link timer, take-over sound).
- **The computer player's fate (claim 8).** `mind::fate` runs for a neutral or special house only (no player house is computer-controlled here): one draw on the simulation RNG over the table the marks select (claim 8's design rule, revision 9, answering Q-1202: an absent mark never selects its table, the others keep their places; the importer warns once for each absent mark when a fate table is non-empty), then hunt. The grinder fate is built with S-0603 (M7a step 3.6: `grinder::grinder_fate`, the nearest own recycling building by ρ₃) and the bio-reactor fate with S-0605 (step 3.5); a failed assignment and the team fate fall back to hunt (teams are not built).
- **Release (claims 9 to 12).** `mind::release` (sound, owner back, guard), `release_all` most recent first from death, removal, erasing hits, trigger owner changes and a controller building out of operation in its own turn (`mind::building_turn`). The quirk switches `quirks.mind_control_owner_change_keeps_victims` (`mind::owner_changed`), `quirks.dying_victim_holds_slot` (`mind::killed`) and `quirks.deploy_makes_control_permanent` (`mind::converted`) are wired; each is tested in both positions.
- **Permanent control and the original owner (claims 13 to 15).** `mind::dominate` (the S-0612 caller is not built), `mind::original_owner` (read by S-0414 claim 5's defeat), deploying through `mind::converted`. Claim 15 (revision 9, answering Q-1200): a vehicle controlled by a controller is not deploy-ready to deploy into a construction yard (`econ::deploy::deploy_ready` reads `mind::controlled_by`, so a permanently controlled one deploys, for the dominating house); the deploy command skips it (`targeting::key_command`), a queued deploy does not survive the takeover's guard reset, and after release it deploys. The refusal cursor of S-0214 table VD row VD2 is built (M8 A4 round 2, `targeting::vehicle_deploy`).
- **Overload (claim 16).** `mind::overload_turn` runs in the unlimited controller's own turn (revision 9, answering Q-1201): `tick::object_turn` runs the controller's `process::head` (the first per-tick start and the berserk countdown) and then the overload update, before sinking, self-healing, radiation, the fall, the temporal step and `process::tail` (the rest of behaviour processing), movement and firing. Moving the controller's first start and countdown ahead of its self-healing is our engine's choice: S-0610 claim 6 and S-0314 claim 9 leave that order to the engine. It runs in exactly the countdown's updates: not while being erased (`temporal::erased`), not in warp-only updates, not out of play. A lethal pulse ends the update. the highlight count, F2's level, the demolition-warhead hit, the overload sound once per run, the spark and rocking draws (the rocking rate is reported as `Effect::OverloadRock` and drawn by the view since M8 A4 rocking).
- **Presentation (claims 17 and 18).** `mind::link_lines` and `mind::ring` are queries. `Session::draw` draws the link lines in the controller house's colour while the timer runs or the controller, its victim or its carrying transport is selected, and a stand-in ellipse for the control and permanent rings (the ring animations are not drawn). The control pips of S-0111 claim 13 are drawn since M8 step A3 intake 46 (`Live::pip_rows`, with `mind::highlight` for the overload pip).
- **Transports.** A vehicle transport takes neither a controlled unit nor a controller holding victims (S-0507 claim 4); a controlled unit that boards is released.
- **Importer.** `InfiniteMindControl` (weapons); `MindClearedSound`, `LeptonMindControlOffset`, `MindControlRingOffset` and `AlternateFLH0` to `AlternateFLH4` (types and art); `[AudioVisual]` take-over, release and overload sounds; `[CombatDamage]` controlled and permanently controlled animation types, the link-line frames, the overload count, damage and frames lists and the `AICapture*` tables and marks. Warnings: a takeover weapon outside the primary slot with no primary, a takeover primary with damage ≤ 0 that is not unlimited, overload lists shorter than the count list.
- **Client and lane.** The local lane scenes `mind` and `overload` (`crates/app/tests/m7a_lane.rs`, screenshots `local-lane/m7a/mind-*.png`). AI lane (map 8, seed 7, Yuri house 1): sides 0,2: Allied won at 16 266, 1 takeover, one controller built, no brain orders; sides 1,2: Soviet won at 37 607, 5 brain orders, 13 takeovers, at most 4 held at once, 9 controllers.

Seams: teams (the grinder and bio-reactor fates are built with S-0603 and S-0605 at M7a steps 3.6 and 3.5), S-0612 (domination's caller), S-0606 (bunkered vehicles), the spark particle systems and the rocking, the ring animation art, the control pips. Interim rules waiting for answers: none (Q-1200 to Q-1203 are answered by revision 9, step 1c; Q-1150 by revision 12, step 1d, which withdrew the switch `quirks.removed_tag_attack_event` that step 1c had wired). The drain-link path (S-0608, `drain_link_formed`) is built at M7b step 3.3.


## Simulation (M7a: Gattling spin-up)

S-0609 (revision 5; built at revision 3, revision 4 taken in at M7a step 1c, [docs/m7-claims/step1c-m7a.md](m7-claims/step1c-m7a.md), revision 5 at step 1d, [docs/m7-claims/step1d-m7a.md](m7-claims/step1d-m7a.md)) is built in `glasnost_sim::gattling`; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0609.md](m7-claims/S-0609.md), the tests are `claims_s0609.rs`, the audio test `spin_sound_draws` and, for the importer, `gattling_spin_settings`. The state is the spin value and gattling stage (`CombatState::spin`, `gattling_stage`), the animation counter (`CombatState::anim_frame`) and the elapsed-tick count (`Behaviours::elapsed`); all are saved and hashed. Save format version 48.

- **Spin-up and spin-down (claims 1 to 4, F1 to F3).** `gattling::spin_up` and `spin_down` apply F1 and F2 with the normal or, while the object is elite, the elite thresholds (an absent elite threshold is 0). The rise test reads the value before the update; there is no clamp. `quirks.gattling_zero_spin_down_empties` (a spin-down of amount 0 empties the value) is wired and tested in both positions.
- **Vehicles (claims 5, 6, 12).** `combat::behave` calls `gattling::vehicle_step` once per firing step with the firing check's answer for slot 0 (no target or an empty slot 0 spins down; the deploy-to-fire exit makes no update). A passenger or an object being erased takes no firing step, so its spin is frozen.
- **Buildings (claims 7 to 9, F4, F5).** `process` counts the elapsed ticks of a gattling building (`gattling::elapse`); its attack processing calls `building_attack` with the answer and multiplier c, a SAM building makes no update, and guard, sticky and area guard processings call `building_guard` (spin-down by c). `gattling::building_turn` runs in the building's own turn after its processing (`tick.rs`): the passive spin-down outside attack more than G + 5 ticks after the last shot, and the animation counter. **Buildings now import `TurretCount`** (S-0308 Import mapping, type section; `BUILDING_FIELDS`), so a gattling building is a multi-weapon type that chooses its weapon by stage.
- **Resets (claim 11).** Boarding resets value, stage and elapsed count (`transport::reset_gattling_spin`, which was a seam); each successful temporal lock, a joining one included, gives one spin-down (`temporal`). Entering an absorbing building resets too (S-0605 claim 7, built at M7a step 3.5).
- **Sound (claim 13).** The simulation reports each update as `Effect::Spin` (`SpinEvent::Up { raised, weapon }` with the slot-2s′ weapon after elite replacement, `Down`, `Left` when the object leaves the map) and marks a gattling firer's shots so they play no per-shot report. The audio crate keeps the playing mark and takes the draws on the unsynchronised stream (`Audio::spin`); an unresolved report list counts as empty. Nothing of this reaches game state.
- **Animation (claim 14).** `anim_frame` advances for a vehicle once per completed firing step with value at least 1, for a building once per tick with positive value after its processing. The original's drawing-only double advance outside attack is not reproduced. The client reads it for a building's turret frame and a vehicle's (`render::voxel::vehicle_turret_frame`).
- **Importer.** `WeaponStages`, `RateUp`, `RateDown`, `Stage1` to `Stage6` and `EliteStage1` to `EliteStage6`, read per layer while the gattling flag is set and the count is at least 2 (`sim_combat::spin_settings`); warnings for a count above 6, negative settings, a final count below 2 after an earlier layer set the thresholds (at the layer that reduced it; a count raised back to 2 or more gives no warning) and empty stage slots 2s.
- **Settings outside the domain (revision 4, answering Q-1220; revision 5, answering Q-1151).** Engine rules with no quirk switch: negative stage counts, amounts and thresholds are used as 0, with an importer warning for each; spin products, sums and the elapsed-tick count saturate at 2³¹ − 1 (`saturation_at_the_32_bit_bound`); and the stage count as the layers finally leave it governs, so a type a later layer lowered below 2 stages uses no thresholds (all 0, though the importer still writes the held rows: the cap is 0, the value never grows, the stage stays 0; `layered_reduction_below_2_uses_no_thresholds`), and the importer warns about the reducing layer (`gattling_spin_settings`).
- **Client and lane.** The local lane scene `gattling` (`crates/app/tests/m7a_lane.rs`, screenshots `local-lane/m7a/gattling-*.png`): a gattling vehicle ordered at an enemy vehicle climbs to its top stage (stage 1 after 200 ticks, stage 2 after 400) and, its target removed, falls back to 0 within 23 ticks; a gattling building beside an enemy vehicle climbs to its top stage through guard and attack. AI lane (map 8, seed 7, Yuri house 1, after merging M7a step 1b): sides 0,2: Allied won at 15 708; the Yuri house had 2 gattling types, ordered 2 gattling defences, kept 12 spins, never had a gattling at its top stage, built one controller and took nothing. Sides 1,2: Soviet won at 13 301; 2 gattling types, 1 defence ordered, 210 spins kept, 218 house-ticks at the top stage, 1 takeover. Replays and mid-game saves matched in both.

Seams: none (the absorption reset is built with S-0605 at M7a step 3.5). Interim rules waiting for answers: none (Q-1220 is answered by revision 4, Q-1151 by revision 5).

## Simulation (M7a: gas clouds)

S-0613 (revision 5 since M7a step 1e, [step1e-m7a.md](m7-claims/step1e-m7a.md); built at revision 4; S-0202 revision 29, S-0010 revision 18, S-0109 revision 29 and S-0203 revision 36, which restate its F9, its loading-time Edge case and the shared system's place, were taken in at step 1f with no behaviour change, [step1f-m7a.md](m7-claims/step1f-m7a.md)) is built in `glasnost_sim::gas`; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0613.md](m7-claims/S-0613.md), the tests are `claims_s0613.rs`, the unit tests in `gas.rs` and, for the importer, `sim_gas_tests.rs`. The state is `World::gas` (`GasWorld`: the particle systems with their particles, the shared system's object number and the virus-death animations); all of it is saved and hashed. Save format version 49.

- **Particle systems as world objects (claims 1, 4, 6, 17).** A particle system takes an object number and a place in the update order; `tick::object_turn` runs `gas::system_turn` for systems and `gas::anim_turn` for virus-death animations, after projectiles. The shared gas system (`GasCloudSys`) is the last object of the game-start step to enter play (`load::new_game`, just before the ore-twinkle setup): after the map-placed objects, the starting units, the crates and every tile or cell animation of loading, so it is appended to the update order after all of them and takes the object number after them (map objects start at 1; since step 1e, in a game with a shared system, every object created in play has a number one higher than before, and so does every particle, which flips F8's parity for them). A particle requested while loading (a μ = 0 tile or cell animation) meets no shared system: its creation draws are taken and no particle is made (Edge cases, a departure; the importer warns for a cell animation, theater tile animations are not seen by the rules import, a seam). A warhead naming a particle system creates it at the end of its detonation (`combat::area::detonate`, S-0303 claim 20) with its first particle. A system of every kind is kept and takes its turns (claim 4); since step 1h the smoke, fire, spark and railgun kinds keep their first particle and run their own turns (S-0616, section below; Q-1243 answered). After the kind's turn work, every system's lifetime goes down by 1 whatever its value (−1 goes on to −2 and below), it is marked to end when the result is exactly 0, and a marked empty system leaves play in the same turn (claim 17). A shared system that leaves play is not created again: later requests take their draws and make no particle (a departure; the importer warns when its lifetime is positive). The gate and lane cloud counters count gas-kind systems only (`gas::is_cloud`).
- **Virus death (claims 2, 3).** `combat::death::infantry_death` calls `gas::virus_death` for row 8 of S-0107 claim 17 and for the extra cases (a not-human type, or a parachuting unit hit by a kind-8 warhead). The animation is a narrow one-pass playback (`VirusAnim`) whose middle event puts its particles into the shared system; the extra cases deliver their middle event at once and one more particle. S-0109's middle event (`load::middle_event`) now delivers its particles into the shared system too.
- **Particles (claims 5 to 15, F1 to F8).** Creation with the S-0202 creation draws (E = (E_max + u) mod 65536, F9); the update pass newest first (on even ticks drift, vertical speed and deflection, then damage, stage and lifetime, claim 18), then the second pass (moves, removals, successors newest dead first). F3's deflection runs in soft single and double precision (`glasnost_num`) through the S-0106 table C4 ramp matrices and is stored as bits. Gas hits go through `combat::damage::apply` with no attacker and the system's house (a berserk warhead therefore works, S-0610's seam) and report `Effect::GasHit`. `quirks.wind_southeast_short` is wired.
- **Importer.** Particle fields `MaxDC`, `Damage`, `Warhead`, `StartFrame`, `StartStateAI`, `EndStateAI`, `StateAIAdvance`, `DeleteOnStateLimit`, `NextParticle`, `NextParticleOffset`, `WindEffect`, `Translucency`; particle-system `BehavesLike`, `HoldsWhat` (absent from a section that is read: the empty-named type, which `layers.rs` registers by reference; a section never read: none; S-0613 revision 6, step 1g), `Lifetime`; General `InfantryVirus`, `WindDirection`. Warnings for a virus animation without a particle type, a wind effect above 10, a wind direction outside 0 to 7 when a type has a wind effect, stage advances of 0 or −1, a shared gas system with a positive lifetime (claim 17), and an overlay cell animation with μ = 0 that requests particles while loading (Edge cases; only when the art lookup ran, since μ needs it).
- **Client.** Every gas particle is drawn as a green placeholder cloud (`Marker::Gas`); claim 16's art, translucency and frame-rate cutoff are not drawn yet. The live view counts gas hits and the most particles at once. Local lane figures from step 3.4, not re-run at step 1e (particles now live longer, F9): scene `gas` (`crates/app/tests/m7a_lane.rs`, screenshots `local-lane/m7a/gas-*.png`): a virus unit's kill leaves the virus animation and up to 3 clouds at once, which deal 2 hits; the install's virus warhead names no particle system. AI lane (map 8, seed 7, Yuri house 1): sides 0,2: Allied won at 16 254; the Yuri house had 1 virus unit; 3 clouds, 25 gas hits, at most 6 particles. Sides 1,2: Soviet won at 13 301 (as before); 1 virus unit, no clouds. Replays and mid-game saves matched in both.
- **Gate.** The duel's gattling top-stage weapon fires a kind-8 warhead naming a cloud system, with a virus animation and the shared system in the fixture; the run counts gas clouds and gas hits (step 3.4: seed 2 had 184 clouds and 274 hits; over seeds 1 to 16 the gas minima were 16 clouds and 26 hits). The change moved some earlier minima (old to new): units made berserk 20 to 25, intent ticks 1 982 to 1 942, slaves at work 6 312 to 6 812, stage rises 9 to 2 (seed 6), top-stage ticks 1 396 to 331, takeover orders 6 to 5, takeovers 10 to 10. A buildable virus infantry type instead lowered more of them (it takes a share of the barracks). At step 1e (revision 5: the shared system's new place, E = E_max + u, the count-down of every lifetime) only `duel-m7a.txt` moved; the goldens were regenerated. Over seeds 1 to 16 the gas minima are now 32 clouds and 141 hits (medians 182 and 717; the clouds live longer with F9). Other minima (step 3.4 to step 1e): units made berserk 25 to 19, intent ticks 1 942 to 1 776, slaves at work 6 812 to 5 537, stage rises 2 to 4, top-stage ticks 331 to 711, takeover orders 5 to 7, takeovers 10 to 8; psychic sensor orders stay 2 in every seed (the known exception).

Seams: claim 16 (drawing detail); laser-fence sections in F3's obstacle test count as inactive; the importer's loading-time warning for theater tile animations. Revisions 7 and 8 (step 1h): owner removal marks a system of every kind, and the shared system stands at S-0616's constant (2688, 2688, 0). Q-1153 and Q-1243 are answered by S-0616 (step 1h). Revision 9 (step 1j): claim 7's one successor is made for a dead particle of any kind in a gas system, the system's kind deciding (S-0616 claim 3; Q-1155 answered). Revision 10 (step 1j): that successor takes the dead particle's speed as well as its velocity, and nothing else (`gas::second_pass`). Q-1240, Q-1241, Q-1242 and Q-1244 are answered by revision 5 (step 1e).

## Simulation (M7a: particle systems)

S-0616 (revision 4, taken in at M7a step 1h, [step1h-m7a.md](m7-claims/step1h-m7a.md)) is built in `glasnost_sim::particles`, only as far as built specs need it; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0616.md](m7-claims/S-0616.md), the tests are `claims_s0616.rs`, the unit tests in `particles.rs`, the creator tests in `claims_s0302.rs`, `claims_s0601.rs` and `claims_s0614.rs` and, for the importer, `particle_system_kind_fields`, `particle_system_kind_warnings`, `default_spark_system_absent_warns` and `particle_quirks_load_in_both_positions`. The systems live with the gas systems in `World::gas`; `gas::system_turn` dispatches on the system's kind and each particle updates and moves by its own kind (claim 3). The kept state (speed, flight direction, spark velocity, fire step, emission timer, spark counter, own stage-advance value, target point, owner object) is saved and hashed. Save format version 52 (54 since step 1j, for the depot memory of S-0314 revision 25 and the fate tick of S-0605 revision 7). Revision 5 (step 1i) and revision 6 (step 1j, [step1j-m7a.md](m7-claims/step1j-m7a.md)) are taken in: since revision 6 successors follow the system's kind, not the particle's (claim 3; Q-1155 answered): one successor in a gas system (S-0613 claim 7, revision 9), claim 8's two in a smoke system, none in a fire, spark or railgun system, for a dead particle of any kind (`claim_3_successors_follow_the_system_kind`). Revision 7 (step 1j): a gas system's successor takes the dead particle's speed and velocity (S-0613 claim 7, revision 10).

- **Creators built (claim 1).** A warhead's system of any kind keeps its first particle (`gas::warhead_system`). The damage smoke of S-0302 claim 13 is a smoke system owned by the damaged object at its position plus the type's damage-smoke displacement (`particles::damage_smoke`, `Object::combat.damage_smoke`); a hit above the yellow ratio marks it and the object keeps it until it leaves play (Q-1153 answered). The overload sparks of S-0601 claim 16 and the drone delivery sparks of S-0614 claim 9 are systems of the game-wide spark type (`particles::spark_system`; none without `[CombatDamage] DefaultSparkSystem`, which the importer warns about).
- **Owner removal (claim 4).** `objects::remove` calls `particles::owner_removed`: every system the object owns loses it and is marked.
- **Kinds (claims 5 to 19, F1 to F4, F8 to F13).** Smoke: update newest first, removal pass with successors (claim 8, F3), move with the smoke wind table and the deck stop (F2), emission and the timer's cut-off (claim 9, F4). Fire: update and step (F8), move, hits on the objects of the cell (F9) with no attacking house. Spark: the burst (F10) and the motion and contact with double gravity (F11). Railgun: the line (F12) once, then marked. The normalised stage-advance value (F13) is formed at creation. Every listed draw is taken, the drawn-only ones included. Single and double precision run in `glasnost_num`.
- **Quirks.** `fire_particle_no_house`, `smoke_ignores_particle_cap`, `spark_double_gravity`, `smoke_deck_extra_update` (each tested both ways) and `flame_sweep_facing` (at M7a a seam that changed nothing; wired at M7b step 3.1, below).

Seams at M7a (all but `electric_bolt_sparks` and `voxel_animation_system` filled at M7b step 3.1, see *Simulation (M7b: particle systems)*; those two at M8 step C5 parts 1 and 2): `effect_weapon_emitter` (fire, spark and railgun emitters of effect weapons, the let-go events, S-0301 claim 5's live-effect gate, the flame sweep F5, the swing F6, the insertion F7, a system's target object), `barrel_particle_system` (S-0303 claim 19 takes its draw only), `destruction_smoke`, `refinery_smoke`, `natural_smoke` (with S-0516's cloak removal and uncloak rule), `electric_bolt_sparks`, `damage_sparks`, `deactivated_sparks`, `voxel_animation_system`, the trigger actions (S-0011 codes 88 and 89), and the drawing of every kind. No object is inside a tunnel, so claim 9's tunnel test always passes. No interim rule is waiting (Q-1155 answered by revision 6, step 1j). Gate (step 1h, after merging main with S-0603, seeds 1 to 16): every minimum and median is the same as on main before the merge (berserk orders 9/19, units made berserk 27/47, sensor orders 2/2, the known exception, intent ticks 1 869/3 316, reveal orders 6/11, reveals fired 6/11, slave-miner buildings 3/5, slave ticks 1 274/7 193, gattling stage rises 6/14, top-stage ticks 448/2 373, takeover orders 3/14, takeovers 7/15, gas clouds 26/147, gas hits 39/487, absorber entry orders 6/15, infantry absorbed 5/8, grinder entry orders 4/8, units ground 4/8); the duel's seed stays 3 (12 000 ticks). The goldens were regenerated.

## Simulation (M7a: Infantry absorber)

S-0605 (revision 2 since step 1g) is built in `glasnost_sim::structures::absorb`; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0605.md](m7-claims/S-0605.md), the tests are `claims_s0605.rs`, two pairs in `pairs_m7a.rs` (a controlled enemy goes into an absorber and comes out an enemy; a released neutral unit takes the bio-reactor fate), the audio test `bio_reactor_sounds` and, for the importer, `absorber_fields`. The state is in `StructState`: a building's passengers in the passenger order, front (the most recent entrant) first (`absorbed`), a passenger's building (`absorbed_in`) the building an actor waits in line for (`waiting`) and the enter-nearest instruction for absorbing buildings (`enter_nearest`, claim 16); an entrant's contact is the ordinary contact field. All of it is saved and hashed. Save format version 51.

- **Acceptance and capacity (claims 1, 2, F1).** `absorb::answer` is the docking answer of S-0214 rows x3, x4, q2 and q3 for an absorbing building (`targeting` reads it, so the *enter* cursor and click follow it): alliance both ways, switched on, the right kind, not balloon-hover, not a controller holding a victim, then F1 (n + 1 ≤ c and size ≤ L, L compared in soft floating point). A building type carries passengers for S-0214 rows x and q when its capacity is above 0; row I13's "carries no passengers" is a capacity equal to 0, so a negative capacity meets neither (claim 1; since S-0214 revision 40, step 1h, for any building type, `targeting::building_passengers`).
- **Contact and waiting in line (claims 3 to 5, 17, F2).** `absorb::order_entered` runs when an *enter* order on an absorbing building is applied (`orders.rs`, after the order's ordinary effects), and the fate of claim 16 and the retry run the same rule (`seek_contact`): a building that holds any contact puts the actor in line with its destination cleared; otherwise it asks for contact (alliance both ways, health above 0, a free link place). Not granted, the actor does not wait and keeps the building as its destination; granted, it asks to load, and a refused loading (switched off) gives the contact up again, the destination kept. `absorb::waiting_turn` is the per-tick retry in the actor's own turn after its movement (`tick.rs`, where the depot retry is; nothing while phasing in): granted and accepted, *enter* queued with an immediate start and the building as destination; refused, contact given up, the none behaviour queued (no-op) and the destination cleared. Link places are max(NumberOfDocks, 1) (`absorb::link_places`). `absorb::process_enter` repeats the loading request at each *enter* processing (`process.rs`); a refusal gives up the contact and falls back to rest with the destination and walk kept, so the entrant walks on and never goes in (scenario A6). A waiting actor's first *enter* processing is the approach (`absorb::approach`): S-0212 claim 25's nearby search from the cell of the building's reference point with table T5's waiting absorber row (the step-off settings, shared as `path::NearbySettings::own_class`: own class with winged as track, own zone, the centre's reach region read for the deck, deck mode on a bridge deck, no height, further or overlay test, no reference cell, so position *N* mod *n*), *move* started at once, the waiting kept; with no cell found it falls back to rest in that processing and keeps waiting (S-0605 revision 5, Q-1152 answered at step 1i; `claim_4_approach_one_free_cell`, `claim_4_approach_tie_by_tick`, `claim_4_approach_no_cell`). Since step 1j (revision 6) a vehicle ordered in to a vehicle absorber always waits in line, even at an empty building: no destination and no contact at the order, then the approach and the retry (`absorb::seek_contact`, `claim_3_vehicle_always_waits_in_line`); since revision 7 claim 16's fate puts a vehicle victim in line the same way, and it sends no contact request in the release tick (`StructState::fate_tick`, `claim_3_vehicle_fate_waits_in_line`); setting up a wait forgets any remembered entry object (in our engine only S-0314 claim 23's depot is one), and an enter order on an absorbing building gives none. Without contact and not waiting the processing falls back at once. A contact ends only in claim 17's cases; `quirks.absorber_stale_contact_kept` (default on) keeps a contact held outside *enter* (off: given up in the holder's turn, the old Q-1267 rule), tested both ways.
- **Going in (claims 6 to 8).** `absorb::arrival` (`walk.rs`) for infantry at the end of a walking step in a cell of the building: one entry raise on the building's tag (S-0524 claim 12), unconditional; without contact nothing more; then the controlled entrant's release (S-0601 claim 9, the release sound before the entry sound), off the map, front passenger, power due when the extra output is above 0, the gattling reset (S-0609 claim 11, `gattling::reset`), the entry sound. `absorb::vehicle_arrival` (`drive.rs`, `hover.rs`) is claim 8's vehicle absorber: the full test on arrival, refused: destination cleared, *guard* queued, a forced scatter. `quirks.absorber_contact_no_capacity_recheck` (default on) is wired and tested both ways.
- **Inside and owners (claims 9, 10).** Passengers are off the map, keep their owner and are left out of the crate infantry count (`crates::infantry_count`); their power (S-0405 claim 1, `econ::power`) follows the building to a new owner.
- **Release (claims 11 to 13, F3).** The deploy click on an absorbing building with passengers gives *unload* (S-0214 row B5 and table M); `absorb::process_unload` lets out the front passenger per processing by S-0507 claim 10's exit search from the top-left cell, rear direction q = (facing + 4) mod 8, exit facing 8192 × e, then a move two cells out; no exit puts the passenger back in front and blocks the others; an empty processing queues *guard* and waits 1 tick. `quirks.absorber_release_second_round_skips_rear` and `quirks.absorber_release_bridge_ends_search` are wired and tested both ways. A voluntary release clears the enter-nearest instruction for a human-controlled owner only (claim 13).
- **Other ways out (claims 14, 15).** Destruction (`absorb::destroyed`, from `combat::death`, S-0312 claim 24: front first, one per foundation cell, the sale's centre placement with one draw per open cell (claim 14); no crew and killing damage that ignores defences kills them), sale (`absorb::sold`, S-0408 claim 8), erasure (`absorb::erased`, S-0514 claim 12: deleted), removal inside (`absorb::removing`: n goes down; `quirks.absorber_removal_keeps_stale_power`, default on, makes no power due so the totals stay stale until something else makes them due; off: power due; tested both ways).
- **Seams filled.** The bio-reactor fate of S-0601 claim 8 (`absorb::bio_reactor_fate`, from `mind::fate`, claim 16: the unit takes the enter-nearest instruction; candidates in the owner's absorber list, the house's holding order `econ.acquired`, each asked claim 1's test; the smallest ρ₃ to the reference point wins, ties to the later one in the list; found: *enter* queued without a start and the destination assigned, which applies claim 3; none: instruction cleared, *hunt*). The instruction repeats the search at every *guard* and *area guard* processing (`absorb::instruction_turn` from `process.rs`, *D*<sub>r</sub>, no draw; S-0215 claim 24 and claim 26 row 3; since S-0215 revision 60, step 1h, answering Q-1154, waiting in line for the building counts as already bound); `quirks.enter_nearest_survives_absorption` (default on) keeps it through an entry, so a neutral passenger let out walks back in (off: entry clears it), tested both ways; the gattling reset of S-0609 claim 11; the absorber entry raise of `mp_triggers`.
- **Importer.** Buildings `InfantryAbsorb`, `UnitAbsorb`, `Passengers`, `SizeLimit` (and the existing `NumberOfDocks`, `ExtraPower`); `[AudioVisual] EnterBioReactorSound` and `LeaveBioReactorSound`, played by the audio crate (`Sound::EnterBioReactor` and `Sound::LeaveBioReactor` reports).
- **Client and lane.** The local lane scene `absorber` (`crates/app/tests/m7a_lane.rs`, screenshots `local-lane/m7a/absorber-*.png`): three infantry clicked onto the install's infantry absorber get contact one at a time (one link place; the others wait in line), the first is inside after 95 ticks, all three after 322, the house's output rises; the deploy click empties it in 31 ticks and the output goes back. The building's passenger animation (S-0108 claim 17) is not drawn; its passenger pips (S-0111 claim 12) are, since M8 step A3 intake 46. AI lane (map 8, seed 7, Yuri house 1): sides 0,2: Allied won at 20 112; the Yuri house had 3 absorbing buildings, gave 4 entry orders, 1 infantry went in. Sides 1,2: Soviet won at 13 305; 2 absorbing buildings, 1 entry order, none went in. Replays and mid-game saves matched in both.
- **Gate.** Alpha's power plant in the M7a duel is BIOP, an infantry absorber (power 50, capacity 5, size limit 1, extra power 40; the fixture's POWR is Beta's only); the run counts entry orders and infantry that went in, and saves and loads at the first tick a passenger is inside. Seed 2: 34 entry orders, 10 absorbed. Minimum / median over seeds 1 to 16 (after merging main at 75350df): berserk orders 6/13, units made berserk 22/29, sensor orders 2/2 (the known exception), intent ticks 1 912/3 740, reveal orders 7/12, reveals fired 7/12, slave-miner buildings 5/8, slave ticks 2 765/8 338, gattling stage rises 4/13, top-stage ticks 868/2 279, takeover orders 7/17, takeovers 11/16, gas clouds 36/110, gas hits 144/404, absorber entry orders 12/20, infantry absorbed 3/7. Every scenario's golden moved from tick 1 (new saved state); the goldens were regenerated. At step 1g (S-0605 revision 2; save format 51, AI game format 20 with the stalled-entrant re-send) the minimum / median over seeds 1 to 16: berserk orders 6/17, units made berserk 20/28, sensor orders 2/2 (the known exception), intent ticks 1 770/3 224, reveal orders 7/12, reveals fired 7/12, slave-miner buildings 6/8, slave ticks 286/6 016, gattling stage rises 4/13, top-stage ticks 461/2 035, takeover orders 3/13, takeovers 10/16, gas clouds 14/100, gas hits 15/404, absorber entry orders 10/18, infantry absorbed 4/8. Without the re-send the infantry-absorbed minimum was 2 (seeds 11, 13 and 14: a contact kept outside *enter*, claim 17). The goldens were regenerated.

Seams: the passenger pips and animation (drawing); teams are still a seam of S-0601 claim 8 (the grinder fate is built with S-0603). Q-1260 to Q-1267 are answered by revision 2 (step 1g). Q-1154 is answered by S-0215 revision 60 (step 1h). Q-1152 is answered by S-0605 revision 5 (step 1i). Revisions 6 and 7 at step 1j. Q-1159 is answered by S-0605 revision 8 (step 1k) as the interim had it: a vehicle still driving from an earlier order is ready at its first start, because a queued *enter* lifts S-0215 claim 11's moving condition, so it makes *enter* current and takes the approach step. Save format 54 since step 1j.

## Simulation (M7a: Grinder)

S-0603 (revision 2) is built in `glasnost_sim::structures::grinder`; the ledger is [docs/m7-claims/S-0603.md](m7-claims/S-0603.md), the tests are `claims_s0603.rs`, two pairs in `pairs_m7a.rs` (a controlled enemy is ground for its controller's house; a released neutral unit takes the grinder fate), the audio test `grinder_consumption_sounds` and, for the importer, `grinder_fields`. No state of its own: an entrant is a unit in *eaten* with the building as its destination, so save format 50 is unchanged.

- **Order and answer (claims 1, 2).** `grinder::answer` is the docking answer of S-0214 table V rows n1 and n2 and row I26 (`targeting` reads it, so the *recycle* cursor and click follow it): one-way alliance, not under construction or being sold, a link place (F2), the naval match, not a balloon, not a loaded vehicle or a controller with a victim, switched on. A building that also absorbs answers by S-0605 claim 1.
- **Approach and consumption (claims 3 to 7).** No contact and no link place; `grinder::process_eaten` is S-0215 claim 28's table for *eaten*. Infantry is consumed at the end of a walking step in a foundation cell (`walk.rs`), a vehicle at the cell-arrival update of the first foundation cell (`drive.rs`, `hover.rs`; occupancy rows VP6 and VP7). The consumption tick: the entry raise, `Effect::Consumed`, the payments, the parasite's host-consumed rule, the entrant's destroyed-by-anything raise, the silent removal of passengers (two levels) and `objects::remove`. `quirks.grinder_no_arrival_recheck` is wired and tested both ways.
- **Payments (claims 8 to 11).** Each consumed object pays the building's owner `sell::refund` of its type with its own owner's factors (a controlled entrant with the controlling house's); health and a harvester's load play no part. No kill, no loss, no `Killed` effect.
- **Interruptions (claim 15).** A new order, a destroyed or sold building and an owner change end the trip (`objects::remove` clears destinations). A path search that fails more than 1 cell from the building clears the destination and queues *guard* (S-0603 revision 4 claims 3 and 15, S-0212 claim 23 row 2; `claim_3_failed_approach_gives_up`). Since step 1k (S-0603 revision 7, S-0215 revision 63) a driving vehicle whose body is still turning counts as moving, so that *guard* waits for the turn to end (`process::moving`).
- **Seams filled.** The grinder fate of S-0601 claim 8 (`mind::grinder_fate`: the nearest own recycling building by ρ₃, ties to the latest placed, *eaten* queued); the recycling consumption of S-0524 claim 12's consuming seam with its passenger removal.
- **Seam left.** Claim 12's *active-1* / *special-1* swap (display only).
- **Own AI.** `glasnost_ai::grinder` (AI game format 21, `Uses.grinds`; 20 on this branch until step 1g took 20): the house builds one grinder, the cheapest it can place, once it holds a unit it would send (an idle controlled enemy vehicle or a badly damaged own unit), queued behind a building already on its line and paid as it goes (`Brain::build_grinder`; until step 6 it waited for a free line and twice its cost in hand, which a house short of money never has). It sends idle controlled enemy vehicles (and controlled enemy harvesters, which do no work for it; controlled infantry go to an absorber with room first), badly damaged own units (at most 30 % strength; vehicles only without a repair depot) and, while credits are below 1 000 and no absorber has room, own infantry at home beyond four. Every order is the *recycle* click; a unit more than 12 cells away is first clicked to a cell 4 cells off the grinder. The base defence leaves units bound for a grinder alone. All numbers are `nocite` tuning. Tests: `crates/deterministic/ai/tests/grinder.rs` (10). Finding (Q-1280, answered by S-0212 revision 49 at step 1i): a unit sent from afar gave up the order on the next tick when buildings stood across its route. This is not a grinder matter. `path::compute` follows S-0212 claims 13 and 13a: on open synthetic ground a straight line of seven 1 × 1 buildings, 20 cells away, can defeat a vehicle's or an infantry unit's move to a cell just behind it, and the recycle order to a grinder behind it. Two cell searches fail; the retries close level-0 and level-1 connections but none at level 2, because there the free part borders the next sector; then the new coarse search fails at level 1, and claim 13's table fails the computation. S-0212's short-line edge case confirmed this as the original's behaviour with `quirks.corridor_retry_no_fallback` on (the default); off, one more cell search without a corridor follows and the line is passed at every alignment (since S-0212 r82, intake 34, the buildings' exemption rectangles pass every such line at every shift, and the two steps stay for strips and ledges). Before r82, in the synthetic geometry of `run_s_shifts_with_the_switch_on`, lines of three and five passed at all eight shifts, the 7-line failed at shifts 0 and 1 and the 9-line at shift 0; since r82 every shift passes. The failed unit gives up (S-0603 claim 3, S-0212 claim 23 row 2): *guard* queued, which *eaten* does not hold back: it becomes current at the first start or late start at which the unit is ready (S-0215 claim 11; S-0603 revision 6, step 1j, answering Q-1157). Because a person's direct click still fails at the default at a strip or a ledge (since r82, not at a line of buildings), the AI keeps its two steps.
- **Client and lane.** The local lane scene `grinder` (`crates/app/tests/m7a_lane.rs`, screenshots `local-lane/m7a/grinder-*.png`): three infantry, an own vehicle and an enemy vehicle under control are clicked in (cursor *recycle* for all five). They are consumed after 98, 99, 104, 116 and 163 ticks, and the house is paid. AI lane (map 8, seed 7, Yuri house 1; counts in `local-lane/m7a/s0603/`): sides 0,2: Allied won at 20 112; sides 1,2: Soviet won at 13 305. After merging step 1g (seed 7): map 8 sides 0,2: Allied won at 16 779; sides 1,2: Soviet won at 13 305; map 2 sides 0,2: Allied won at 18 913; sides 1,2: Yuri won at 29 979. All four: 0 grinder entries, 0 units ground, no recycling building. (Before the merge, map 2 sides 1,2 built one at about 16 500 and ground 1 unit.) Why, traced with a temporary probe of `Brain::build_grinder`: the stock recycling building is offered (`side-audit 2 8 7` orders it at 6 915 in the sandbox), and from 4 200 every condition holds but the money. Until about 4 600 to 5 100 the building line is busy with plan buildings while the grinder is affordable. After that, credits stay below twice its cost whenever the line is free (map 2 sides 1,2: from 6 900 to the end of the game). On map 8 sides 0,2, by the time money came back the house had lost a prerequisite building. So credits and the plan's use of the line decide it. No rule of ours stops a build a person would make, and the AI was not changed for it. Outputs: `ai-sides-0-2.txt`, `ai-sides-1-2.txt`, `ai-sides-0-2-map2.txt`, `ai-sides-1-2-map2.txt` (money values removed). Replays and mid-game saves matched in both.
- **Gate.** The M7a duel's Alpha may build GRND (2 × 2, cost 400, no power; Alpha only), and starts with four HULKs north of its start (an unarmed vehicle of the fixture that no house can build), placed at health 51 of 256, which a person would recycle. The run counts grinder entry orders and units ground (`Effect::Consumed`), and saves and loads at the first tick a unit is bound and the first consumption. Seed 3 (`M7A_SEED`, unchanged from step 1g): 4 entry orders, 4 ground, every other counter at least 3 except sensor orders 2. Minimum / median over seeds 1 to 16, measured after merging step 1g:
  - berserk orders 9/19, units made berserk 27/47;
  - sensor orders 2/2, intent ticks 1 869/3 316;
  - reveal orders 6/11, reveals fired 6/11;
  - slave-miner buildings 3/5, slave ticks 1 274/7 193;
  - gattling stage rises 6/14, top-stage ticks 448/2 373;
  - takeover orders 3/14, takeovers 7/15;
  - gas clouds 26/147, gas hits 39/487;
  - absorber entry orders 6/15, infantry absorbed 5/8;
  - grinder entry orders 4/8, units ground 4/8.

  Without the hulks the grinder counters missed the margin (before step 1g: minimum 0, median 3): after the grinder stands (tick 6 200 to 7 400) Alpha seldom has an idle controlled vehicle, nothing at or below 30 % health, and no surplus infantry at home. Letting Beta build GRND as well was tried and dropped. With the hulks, 8 of the 16 games end before 12 000 ticks (Alpha wins seeds 1, 2, 7, 8, 9, 12, 14 and 15); seed 3 plays the full run. The AI duels' goldens moved with the AI game format and were regenerated after the merge.
